- `2026-09-01T18:45:00Z` step 3: small run, done directly without subagent dispatch (source-access-triage read as house procedure; no rung of its ladder was needed — every witness below returned HTTP 200 on first request). Worked lead (4) of the Kabbalah-leads INBOX entry, the one the ~18:00 cycle had confirmed reachable: Sefer Yetzirah. Captured chapter 1 COMPLETE (the whole of the book's treatment of the ten Sefirot Belimah; chapters 2-6, on the letters, deliberately not captured) from FOUR witnesses so that no quoted phrase rests on one transcription — two Hebrew digitizations collated verse by verse (Sefaria's Warsaw 1884 text from the National Library of Israel copy NNL_ALEPH001310968, public domain; and Hebrew Wikisource `ספר יצירה א` rev. 2870897 of 2024-08-04, a different recension) plus two independent English renderings (the Sefaria Community Translation, CC0, and Isidor Kalisch's published translation of 1877, public domain, from Internet Archive scan 1877-kalisch-sepher-yezirah). Collation recorded in the capture rather than smoothed over: 1:2, 1:8, 1:9 and 1:10 identical word for word; the three verses this repository quotes (1:4, 1:5, 1:7) identical apart from orthography (beli mah as one word or two, plene/defective spelling, we-hashev/we-hoshev); substantive divergence only from 1:11 onward, where the recensions differ and from which nothing is quoted. Kalisch divides the chapter into 9 sections against the others' 14 mishnayot and that mapping is recorded (1:7 = his §6). Wrote raw/202609011825-sefer-yetzirah-chapter-1.txt, reference/sefer-yetzirah--202609011825, literature/sefer-yetzirah-counts-ten-from-the-spirit-out-to-the-six-directions--202609011830, and permanent/the-sefirot-vocabulary-does-not-begin-as-articulation-within-god--202609011840. The atomic claim is a CHECK on this base's own construction rather than a corroboration of it: Sefer Yetzirah enumerates its ten as a descent — God's spirit, then wind, water, fire, then the six directions — under a Lord who "has no second" and is not one of them, so the intra-divine reading of the sefirot that permanent 202609011543 relies on is a later development of the vocabulary and cannot borrow the word's antiquity. 202609011543 amended in place with that limit and a reciprocal `elaborates` edge

PDF page 5 was therefore rendered to a raster image and read directly, and the capture records the PRINTED text with all eight OCR divergences listed in its header. Four touch the physics: '7.65 Me V' for '7.65 MeV', '7 .12 MeV level in 160' for the 7.12 MeV level in 16O, 'nucleosyhthesis', and 'put-Lip' for 'put-up'. A note quoting the OCR layer would have misnamed the nuclide
- `2026-09-03T00:01:26Z` step 3 (capture fidelity, Polkinghorne): both extractors agree on the words but NOT on reading order -- pdfminer.six spliced the two columns of print p. 2, which would have placed the multiverse paragraph on p. 2 and inverted the two halves of the natural-theology paragraph. Pages 1-3 were rendered and read to fix the true order and per-page locators; poppler's order was correct. The literature note's locators are the verified ones

The only Islamic primary text is the Qur'an capture, which is scripture and not anyone's argument; Saritoprak reaches the base only secondhand as a person quoted in the NPR piece, with no reference note and nothing captured. The Jewish side by contrast is argued from inside across six captured sources (Maimonides, Ramban, Shulchan Arukh with the Rema, Tiqqunei ha-Zohar, Sefer Yetzirah, Dweck on Modena). A concrete and cheap piece of the gap: raw/202608311923 holds Surah 112, 4:171 and 5:73 -- oneness, "say not 'Three'", and "they surely disbelieve who say Allah is the third of three", all three marking DIFFERENCE -- and omits 29:46, "our God and your God is One", which is the most on-point verse in the Qur'an for this question and runs the other way. raw/ is immutable so that needs a new capture, not an edit. The inquiry sets the test for answering it as whether 202608311942, the base's most-linked note, has to change, rather than whether a Muslim source was added
- `2026-09-03T21:25:00Z` gap 2 (filed as inquiry 202609032123, high): `zettelkasten method` is a CONFIGURED topic whose grounding contains no empirical evidence of any kind. The five sources under the topic are a how-to preview (Ahrens), the practitioner (Luhmann), the practitioner's archivist (Schmidt), a 1686 method (Locke) and a historian of the practice (Blair): four kinds of witness and not one test. The inquiry names Mueller & Oppenheimer 2014 together with its 2019 replication failure (citing the headline alone would be the exact error this base exists to avoid), retrieval practice, and transactive memory -- and states explicitly that "almost no direct evidence exists" is a legitimate answer to be written as a limiting note, not a failed cycle

It does, and it now carries a limit. A Common Word answers the same-God question by taking neither horn of the reference/description grid: it concedes descriptive divergence outright ("Muslims recognize Jesus Christ as the Messiah, not in the same way Christians do"), presupposes rather than argues co-reference, and puts a shared OBLIGATION where an answer would go. The sharpest evidence that it is not claiming co-reference is that the "common word" it offers is 3:64, one of whose three clauses is "we shall ascribe no partner unto Him" -- the tradition's own exclusion formula, the same idiom 4:171 and 5:73 turn against saying "Three". A document arguing for a shared referent would not build its common ground out of that

- `2026-09-04T02:10:00Z` step 3 (the finding). The inquiry set its own test: establish whether the general note-taking evidence is contested and whether any of it reaches the slip-box claims, and said explicitly that "almost none, and here is why that matters" would be a real answer rather than a failed cycle. Both halves came out. (a) The famous 2014 longhand-over-laptop effect did NOT survive direct replication: "performance did not consistently differ between any groups", and among those groups was one that took no notes at all; the replication's own meta-analysis found "small (nonsignificant) effects favoring longhand"; the authors conclude that saying which method is superior "seems premature". (b) None of it reaches a slip-box claim — the experiments manipulate how a person writes during ONE LECTURE and score a test taken shortly after, while this base's claims are about accumulation, linking, and a later self, over years. Mismatch of construct, not shortage of studies. The unexpected result is where the one point of contact turned out to be: not any slip-box claim, but the house rule that a literature note must be in the reader's OWN WORDS, which is near enough the mechanism Mueller and Oppenheimer proposed — and whose evidential state is exactly the suspension permanent 202609040210 describes

- `2026-09-04T05:20:00Z` step 3 (the finding, and it is not the one the INBOX entry predicted): the entry expected the audit either to upgrade permanent 202609040340's robustness paragraph or to show the claim weaker than the survey implied. Both happened, and a third thing did. (1) The review is NOT a meta-analysis — its authors considered pooling and refused, on non-independent effect sizes and low meta-analytic reproducibility (accepted ms. p. 7) — so Karpicke's 2025 survey mis-describes one of the three audits it names, and literature 202609040330 now carries that correction. (2) The distribution is less uniform than the 57% headline: 16 large, 12 medium, 18 small or very small, 3 negative, and 24 further comparisons unscorable because the original papers under-reported. (3) THE REAL FINDING: classroom effect sizes were LARGER at a 1-3 day delay and SMALLER at end-of-semester (p. 35), which the reviewers themselves flag as the reverse of the laboratory gradient and make their first recommendation for future research (p. 43). That bears directly against the extrapolation in 202609040340 and is written as its own permanent note 202609040515, typed `contradicts` into 202609040340 and narrowed in its own body to the step it actually touches — delay was coded across studies, never manipulated within one, so no causal claim about delay survives it

- `2026-09-04T07:05:00Z` step 3 (provenance, done mechanically): the API names no printed edition, so no edition is claimed and every locator is by sura:aya. Each of six quoted Arabic strings was normalised (diacritics, tatweel, alef/ya/ta-marbuta forms and punctuation stripped) and substring-matched against text served by hosts INDEPENDENT of Quran.com: quran.ksu.edu.sa (King Saud University) and tafsir.app. The 29:46 strings matched on both; the 3:64 strings matched on tafsir.app, the KSU URL for sura 3 returning a 203-byte stub which is recorded as a failed witness rather than counted. Nothing is quoted on one host's authority. Also recorded for the next run: `/api/v4/quran/tafsirs/15?verse_key=…` returns an EMPTY list for a verse the text exists for; `/api/v4/tafsirs/15/by_ayah/<s>:<a>` is the endpoint that works, and the emptiness of the first must not be read as the text being unavailable
- `2026-09-04T07:05:00Z` step 3 (the findings, and the second one cuts against this base's own note): (1) On 29:46 al-Tabari glosses "our God and your God is one" as "and the one we worship and the one you worship is one" — moving from ilah to ma'bud, which is the identity question — and spends NO argument on it, in a passage where he canvasses five readings of a neighbouring clause, weighs them, answers an objection and refuses an abrogation claim. The absence of argument is the finding. (2) Therefore the 2007 letter's silence about co-reference is NOT an inherited absence: the tradition's foundational commentary states co-reference flatly. Permanent 202609040115 is amended in place, its form-half strengthened and its available-explanation half withdrawn. (3) On 3:64 the "common word" is glossed as an EQUITABLE word whose content is tawhid plus disavowal of every rival object of worship, and the occasion reports transmit it with its refusal attached ("they refused to accept this or the other"). So the verse A Common Word takes its name from is, classically, a summons that was declined — the letter's use is a re-reading, not a continuation. (4) The passage that affirms a shared object of worship also endorses reading "except those of them who do wrong" as those who withhold the jizya, "those you dispute with by the sword". Shared referent and juristic subordination, in one passage, with no tension registered. That composite is the shape neither side of the modern debate expects, and it is why note 202609040710 states a limit rather than an irenic result
- `2026-09-04T07:05:00Z` step 3 (what is owed and stated rather than hidden): every English rendering in the literature and permanent notes is THIS REPOSITORY'S OWN working translation of the captured Arabic — no published translation was consulted — and each rendering that carries weight is printed beside the Arabic so it can be checked. The limit is stated in the reference note, the literature note and this log line rather than in one place

The Arabic normaliser first used here was built on the class `[ؐ-ًؚ-ٰٟۖ-ۭـ]`, whose first range (U+0610-U+064B) contains the whole letter block U+0621-U+064A, so it deleted letters as well as marks. It surfaced when a passage visible by eye reported zero hits; `norm('ضاهوهم')` returned the empty string. Every match in this cycle was re-run under a normaliser that strips marks by explicit code point. The substantive results did not change. Filed as an INBOX entry at priority high, with a rule: a normaliser used to establish an ABSENCE must print a positive and a negative control in the same run

He knows his own school's *naskh* reports, quotes al-Baqir via Zurara and al-'Ayyashi, and answers them with the same objection: `ولا يجوز تقدم الناسخ على المنسوخ`. So the Imami half of the base's claim is now SPLIT exactly as the Zaydi half was, and the qualifier al-Sabuni gave the Zaydis was owed to the Imamiyya too. Two further results: he supplies from inside the Shi'i tradition the argument from Qur'anic usage the base held only from al-Qurtubi and al-Sabuni, and adds the distinction they lack — `غير أن إطلاق الفعل غير إطلاق الوصف والتسمية به`, doing shirk is not being named a mushrik, with a Muslim who skips the *hajj* as the parallel; and the permanence restriction SURVIVES his rejection of its grounds, rebuilt as *takhsis* (`مخصصاً متقدماً`) rather than *naskh*, with the *mut'a* permission grounded in `وقد عمل بها الأصحاب`. 29 quotations checked mechanically against the capture, controls in the same pass per the house rule (`norm('المشركات')` survives; `أهل الكتاب` counts 30 in Q 5:5; negative control `الزيدية` 0 in both sections); 28 hit first time and the single miss was a drafting slip — a phrase attributed to the wrong section — corrected in the note rather than dropped

- `2026-09-04T16:23:00Z` step 8 (gates, ADDENDUM — a defect this cycle inflicted on itself and caught): running `verify_refs.py --offline --no-render` locally as a "CI rehearsal" is DESTRUCTIVE and must not be done in a working tree. It does not merely check offline; it REWRITES each reference's `verification` block to what it could establish offline, so 20-odd references that had `method: raw-capture+openlibrary` / `raw-capture+crossref` with `identifier_check: confirmed` were downgraded in place to bare `raw-capture` and lost the confirmation. CI is unaffected because it runs against a throwaway checkout and commits nothing. Caught by reading the diff of files this cycle had no business touching rather than by any gate — every gate was clean on the degraded state, which is the point: the lints do not care that a live registry confirmation was replaced by a weaker one. Repaired by re-running the LIVE `verify_refs.py --mailto`, after which all richer methods and `identifier_check: confirmed` are back (`git diff` over reference/ now shows zero removed confirmations) and the count is again 74/74. Rule for later cycles: rehearse CI by reading `gates.yml`, never by running its verify step against the working tree
